## ADDED Requirements

### Requirement: the modified-block integrity engine reports replacement fidelity
A pure shared helper SHALL compare a MODIFIED operation's old store block against its replacement (both captured at the projection's own parse snapshot — `buildProjection` additionally returns the raw old/new block text pairs per MODIFIED operation; no consumer re-reads files) and report structural fidelity, WITHOUT changing any verdict, exit code or write semantics. Structure scan: the requirement heading line never participates; requirement prose runs from the line after the heading to the first VALID scenario heading (state A's actual regex `/^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/m`, fence-aware exactly like `stripFences` — only lines inside CLOSED triple-backtick pairs are not delimiters; an unclosed opener is ordinary text and later headings still split); fence delimiter lines and fenced non-empty lines are compared as body lines. Pairing runs per key (scenario ID when present, normalized full title otherwise) through one cardinality truth table — o=0,n=1 added; o=1,n=0 dropped; o=1,n=1 paired (same normalized title → retained, different → titleChanged, mutually exclusive, both body-compared); any side >1 → ambiguous (side old/new/both with counts; the key skips every other class and body comparison). Line comparison is ONE algorithm: order-preserving greedy subsequence over normalized non-empty lines (CRLF/CR→LF, trailing whitespace stripped, leading whitespace stripped; duplicates consumed by count); unmatched OLD lines are `missingLines` (requirement prose misses carry scenario=null; only old-content loss is reported). An idempotent replacement (identical trimmed text) yields an entry whose diff arrays are all empty.

#### Scenario: AM-43 the cardinality truth table classifies every occurrence
- WHEN an old block carries KV-01/KV-02/KV-03 and the replacement carries KV-01 (same title), KV-03 (changed title), KV-04 — and separately each of the eight o/n cardinality rows is exercised (including a duplicated KV-05 on one side and no-ID title keys)
- THEN retained=[KV-01], titleChanged=[KV-03 with old/new titles], dropped=[KV-02], added=[KV-04]; each truth-table row lands in its one class; ambiguous entries carry {key, side, oldCount, newCount} and skip every other class; ambiguous ordering is old-present keys by old first occurrence, then new-only keys by new first occurrence

#### Scenario: AM-44 the subsequence comparison reports lost lines faithfully
- WHEN a retained scenario's replacement drops one AND clause, reorders two lines, repeats a line fewer times, differs only in indentation/trailing whitespace on another, and the requirement prose loses a sentence
- THEN missingLines lists the dropped AND clause, the reordered-out line and the under-repeated line (by count) with full original text, the whitespace-only difference is NOT reported, and the prose loss carries scenario=null

#### Scenario: AM-45 structure boundaries follow state A exactly
- WHEN a rename-then-modify replacement preserves prose and scenarios verbatim under the new requirement name, a CLOSED fence contains a scenario-looking heading, an UNCLOSED fence precedes a real scenario heading, and a heading uses multiple spaces after `####`
- THEN the rename case reports zero missingLines (the heading line is never compared); the fenced fake heading does not split a scenario and its lines are body-compared; the heading after the unclosed opener still splits (stripFences strips only closed pairs); the multi-space heading is a valid delimiter

#### Scenario: AM-46 archive prints the integrity section without changing its semantics
- WHEN `apriori archive --change <name>` (the high-level form only; the single-file form is out of scope) passes preflight with a MODIFIED delta whose replacement drops a scenario — on dry-run and on `--write`
- THEN the same human integrity section prints (before any write on `--write`), the RESULT line, exit code and written bytes are identical to a run without the report, and any preflight failure (malformed, CAS, conflict, denial) prints NO integrity section

#### Scenario: AM-47 the archive id-pattern channel is resolved, terminable and degradable
- WHEN archive runs with a custom config `id-pattern` row recognizing letter-suffixed IDs; with no row (default); with an uncompilable row; and with an injected matcher failure
- THEN the first two produce correct reports through the injected terminable matcher (bin wires it in; archive-merge itself never requires spec-runner); the last two print exactly one stderr warning — the WHOLE line `warning: modified-integrity <reason>` passed through sanitizeMsg (control chars replaced with `·`, ≤200 UTF-16 units) — skip the report, and leave every other archive output and exit unchanged

## ADDED Requirements

### Requirement: verify --change carries the modified-block integrity report
On a trustworthy `--change` run, `verify` SHALL attach the integrity engine's report for every MODIFIED operation (rename-then-modify targets included, keyed by the post-rename name with the pre-rename block as baseline). The old blocks' scenario titles ride the EXISTING single title batch as a fourth tag class that never enters binding accumulation, scenario counts or the change scope — the two-batch matcher contract stands. JSON: `modifiedIntegrity` is ALWAYS present on GREEN/GAPS (one entry per MODIFIED operation, empty-diff entries included; `file` = store-relative suffix; element shapes, value choices and orderings per the engine's contract; missingLines carry full untruncated text) and ABSENT on every ERROR class and on every `--specs` run (byte goldens continue to prove the latter). Human: a `— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —` section prints an entry when any of dropped/missingLines/ambiguous/titleChanged is non-empty (`!` prefix on dropped/missingLines/ambiguous lines; a plain one-line note per titleChanged; counts only for retained/added), stays entirely silent when every entry's four classes are empty, and truncates lines the state-A way (`slice(0,119)+'…'`). The report never changes the verdict or the exit code.

#### Scenario: SR-65 the report rides the run without touching the verdict
- WHEN a `--change` run's MODIFIED replacement drops KV-02 and loses one AND line, while every scoped scenario is bound green (TAP covering the replacement's scenarios, exit 0)
- THEN the run is still GREEN exit 0; `modifiedIntegrity` carries dropped=[KV-02] and the missing line with full text; the human section prints them with `!`; and removing one scoped test flips the same run to GAPS exit 1 with the identical report attached

#### Scenario: SR-66 presence follows the outcome class
- WHEN `--change --json` runs end GREEN, GAPS, pre-test ERROR and post-TAP ERROR, and a `--specs --json` run executes
- THEN GREEN/GAPS carry `modifiedIntegrity` (an ADDED-only delta yields `[]`; an idempotent MODIFIED yields its empty-diff entry); both ERROR classes and the `--specs` run omit the field entirely (absent, never null)

#### Scenario: SR-67 the old-block titles join the one batch and bind nothing
- WHEN a config-origin `--change` run with a MODIFIED delta executes with a counting child seam
- THEN the matcher child still runs EXACTLY twice, the first payload contains the OLD block's titles alongside the projection and sibling titles, and the old titles appear in no binding class, no scenario count, no change scope and no store report

#### Scenario: SR-68 the frozen live-specimen fixture reports exactly as hand-derived
- WHEN the engine runs over the frozen copy of the verify-change-scope change's real MODIFIED block (the nine-scenario `projected verify` requirement) against its frozen old store block
- THEN the report deep-equals the hand-derived expectation: retained = the seven untouched scenarios, titleChanged = SR-16 and SR-18 (with their old/new titles), dropped=[], added=[], ambiguous=[], and missingLines exactly as hand-listed from the two rewritten scenario bodies
# design — modified-block-integrity

## G1 引擎（lib/archive-merge.js，纯共享 helper——r2 advisory 约束）

- `buildProjection` 增量返回 `modifiedBlocks: Map<suffix, [{name, oldBlock, newBlock}]>`——在其 merge 处捕获（MODIFIED 命中 store 时 old=store.get(name) 替换前文本、new=delta 块；rename-then-modify：old=改名前 A 块文本（RENAMED 步骤替换 key 前捕获）、name=G；idempotent unchanged 路径同样捕获（等文本对））。单快照纪律：一切文本来自 buildProjection 已读内容。
- `compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf)`：`idOf(title) → id|null` 由调用方注入（引擎零 matcher/零 fs）。内部：
  1. `scanBlockStructure(text)`：剥标题行；按 state-A 正则 + 闭合 fence 对感知切出 [prose, ...scenarios{title, bodyLines}]；未闭合开栏行按普通文本。
  2. 基数真值表分类（键 = idOf(title) ?? normalizedTitle；先各侧计数）。
  3. 保序贪心子序列（normalize：CRLF/CR→LF、去行尾空白、剥行首空白、跳过空行；Map<line, 消费队列> 实现按序消费）。
  4. 返回 `{retained, titleChanged, dropped, added, ambiguous, missingLines}`（排序按 req B4：旧块序/新块序/ambiguous 两段式）。
- 导出 `compareModifiedBlock` 与 `formatIntegrityHuman(entries)`（human 段渲染共用——verify 与 archive 同段式；显著性规则与 119+… 截断在此实现）。

## G2 verify 面（lib/spec-runner.js）

- collectChangePairs 时从 `b.deltaOps` 已知哪些 suffix 有 MODIFIED；`b.modifiedBlocks`（buildChangeProjection 透传）供旧块——旧块场景标题作为第 4 类 pairs（`kind:'integrity'`）并入同一标题批：makeThenBind 扩展为接受分段（binding pairs / sibling titles / integrity titles），返回各段 ids；binding acc 只由第一段构成（scenarioCount/changeScope/storeReport 不受染——change 2 的全量 deep oracle 是护栏）。
- applyChangeScope 后（GREEN/GAPS 路径）：以批回的 old-title→id 查表构造 `idOf`，逐 MODIFIED 调引擎 → `run.modifiedIntegrity`；ERROR 路径与 --specs 恒不赋值（Object.hasOwn 护栏沿用）。
- verifyJson：`if (run.modifiedIntegrity) json.modifiedIntegrity = run.modifiedIntegrity`（GREEN/GAPS 恒在——含 []）。formatReport：`extras.modifiedIntegrity` → `formatIntegrityHuman`。
- 新块标题已在投影批内（scoped pairs）——其 id 同表可查；无需额外批。

## G3 archive 面（lib/archive-merge.js cli + bin/apriori.js）

- `archiveChange(o)` 增可选 `o.idMatcherFactory`：`(cwd) => {batch(titles)→{ids}|{failure}} | {error}`——**bin 层注入**：`case 'archive'` 处组装（require spec-runner 的 resolveIdPattern+makeIdMatcher，config>default，无 flag），archive-merge 模块自身零新依赖。
- preflight 全过后（`preflightFailures` 为空、打印 per-module 列表之后、写入之前）：对 `p.modifiedBlocks` 全部条目——工厂缺席/解析失败/matcher.batch failure → stderr 一行 `sanitizeMsg('warning: modified-integrity ' + reason)` 后跳过；成功 → 一次 batch（旧+新块全部标题）→ idOf → 引擎 → `out.push(formatIntegrityHuman(...))`。RESULT 行、exit、写入字节零变化。
- 注入失败的 reason 类：`id-pattern resolution failed: …` / `matcher failure: …`。

## G4 文档

- docs/cli.md/cli_cn.md：verify 节（modifiedIntegrity 字段+human 段）、archive 节（integrity 段+warning 降级）；RUNBOOK.md/RUNBOOK_cn.md 在"MODIFIED 整块替换"警告句旁加一句"（`verify --change`/`archive` 会打印机械保真报告——丢失的场景与子句逐条列出）"；CHANGELOG 条目。

## 测试布局

- `test/modified-integrity.test.js`（新）：AM-43..47 + SR-65..68。引擎单测直接 require；verify 面走 CLI/模块混合；archive 面 CLI（bin 注入路径）+ 注入失败用 seam。
- AC7 fixture：`test/fixtures/modified-integrity/`（old-block.md、new-block.md——冻结复制自 change 2 的真实材料）；期望值**人工推导**写死。
- human golden：一个含全类的 --change 文本输出快照断言（段内）。
- 基数真值表八行表驱动。
# proposal — modified-block-integrity

**WHY**：RUNBOOK 明警"MODIFIED 整块替换会静默删除未重述内容"，但保真性今天全靠 LLM 评审注意力（复盘 P0-3：一次替换靠评审员逐行子序列比对才确认 11/13 行未丢——防线表唯一 ⚠ 项"建议做进 CLI"）。本批 change 2 自己就重述过一个 9 场景块。确定性问题不该交给注意力。

**WHAT**：
1. 纯共享引擎 `compareModifiedBlock`（archive-merge 内，零 matcher 依赖）：结构扫描（fence 感知、state-A 正则、requirement 标题行不比较）→ occurrence 级基数真值表分类（retained/titleChanged/dropped/added/ambiguous）→ 保序贪心子序列行比较（missingLines，含 requirement 散文与 fence 内容）。
2. `verify --change`：报告并入输出（JSON `modifiedIntegrity` GREEN/GAPS 恒在 + human 显著性段）；旧块标题搭乘既有单一标题批（零新增 matcher 调用）；一切 ERROR 类无报告。
3. `archive --change`（high-level form 专属）：dry-run 与 --write preflight 打印同一 human 段；matcher 经 bin 层注入（config>default，可终止通道）；失败降级为整行 sanitizeMsg warning + 跳过报告，archive 语义分毫不动。
4. **informative 定位**：不改任何 verdict/exit/写入语义——机械检查无法区分"有意删"与"手滑丢"，它的职责是把差异摆上台面给人与 P8 评审。
5. docs/cli + RUNBOOK 双语、CHANGELOG。

**OUT OF SCOPE**：single-file archive form；archive --json；确认/override flag；跨块移动检测；自动补回；三个 change 之外的一切语义。

**验收锚点**：req-final AC1–AC10；AC7 活教材冻结 fixture（change 2 的 9 场景重述，人工推导期望值）。

- [ ] T2 红测试：AM-43..AM-45（引擎：真值表八行表驱动、子序列各例、结构边界四例）+ SR-68（fixture 期望）

- [ ] T5 实现 verify 面：pairs 第 4 类、idOf 查表、run.modifiedIntegrity、json/human；SR 全绿 + change 2 全量 oracle 仍绿
- [ ] T6 红测试：AM-46..AM-47（archive 面五路径 + warning 全串 oracle + 字节不变）

# req-final — modified-block-integrity：MODIFIED 整块替换的保真性成为机械报告

target lineage: main（v4 产品线；不合并 v1/v3——两条 legacy 产品线分支）。**state A 声明（REQ-8）**：本 change 构建在工作区中两个未归档先行 change（gate-id-pattern：id-pattern 解析与可终止 matcher；verify-change-scope：change-scoped verdict、单标题批、deltaOps、storeReport）之上；三者按序归档，spec/design 以工作区代码为 state A。

## 背景（现状 A）

同 req-v1（复盘 P0-3 唯一 ⚠ 防线项；活教材 = verify-change-scope delta 的 9 场景块重述；merge() 整块替换零对比；对比原料在 buildProjection 处零成本在手）。

## 目标（目标态 B）

### B1. 保真报告的判定算法（REQ-1/REQ-2 收口）

**规范化**：行统一 CRLF/CR→LF；比较前每行去行尾空白、行首空白（含 tab）整体剥离；空行不参与比较。

**occurrence 级场景配对（唯一基数真值表，REQ-2 r2 收口）**：配对键 = 场景 ID（有 ID 时）或规范化全标题（无 ID 时），同一张表对两类键统一适用。对每个键统计旧块出现数 o、新块出现数 n：

| o | n | 分类 |
|---:|---:|---|
| 0 | 1 | added |
| 1 | 0 | dropped |
| 1 | 1 | 正常配对（有 ID 键：同规范化标题→retained，异→titleChanged，两类互斥且都做体行比较；标题键：即 retained） |
| 0 | >1 | ambiguous(side=new) |
| >1 | 0 | ambiguous(side=old) |
| >1 | 1 | ambiguous(side=old) |
| 1 | >1 | ambiguous(side=new) |
| >1 | >1 | ambiguous(side=both) |

ambiguous 的键**完全跳过** retained/titleChanged/dropped/added 与体行比较；每键一条 entry，携带 side 与两侧计数。

**行级比较（顺序保持子序列，唯一算法）**：对每个 retained/titleChanged 场景，取旧场景体的规范化非空行序列 O 与新体序列 N，做**保序贪心子序列匹配**（O 的每行按序在 N 中找首个未被消费的相等行；重复行按出现次数逐一消费）——未匹配的旧行即 `missingLines`（重排导致的失配如实报告；子序列不要求连续）。

**结构扫描规则（REQ-10 收口）**：① Requirement 标题行（`### Requirement: …`）**永不**参与比较（rename-then-modify 不得因标题差异假报缺失）；② requirement 散文 = 标题行之后第一行起、至第一个**有效**场景标题前，同规则比较，缺行记 `missingLines`（scenario=null，仅报旧内容缺失）；③ 场景体 = 有效场景标题之后至下一个有效场景标题或块尾；④ 有效场景标题沿用 state A 的**实际正则**（`/^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/m`——多空格/tab 均可），fence 感知与 `stripFences` 完全一致——仅**闭合的**三反引号对内的行不是分隔符；⑤ fence 定界行与围栏内非空行都作为当前散文/场景体的比较行；⑥ 未闭合 fence 沿用 state A 实况（`stripFences` 只剥闭合对）：未闭合的开栏行是普通文本，其后的场景标题**照常切分**——与 verify 的场景采集逐字一致（REQ-10 r3 修正：v3 的'吞尾'表述系对 state A 的误判，废止）。

### B2. 消费面与执行纪律（REQ-4/REQ-5 收口）

- **`verify --change`**：旧块标题作为第 4 类 tag 并入**既有的单一标题批**（不新增 matcher 调用——SR-63 的两批契约保持；这些 pairs 不进入绑定 acc，仅供 integrity 配对）；invalid pattern / matcher failure / projection failure 走既有 ERROR 路径，此时**无** integrity 报告。id-pattern 解析与 state A 完全一致（flag > config > default）。
- **`archive --change`（high-level form 专属；single-file form 明确 out of scope——一模块手术是专家手动路径）**：dry-run 与 `--write` preflight 输出同一数据语义的 human 段（**不新增 --json**，REQ-3 附带裁定）。id 提取沿用统一解析（config > default，archive 无 flag）与可终止通道——经 **bin 层依赖注入**（bin/apriori.js 同时持有两模块，把 spec-runner 的 matcher 工厂注入 archive cli 的可选参数；archive-merge 模块自身仍零依赖 spec-runner，方向不破）。invalid config pattern 或 matcher failure → 打印一条 bounded warning、**跳过 integrity 报告**，archive 的一切既有语义（exit/写入/输出其余部分）不变。
- **`gate`**：不消费。

### B3. 报告存在性矩阵（REQ-6 收口）

| 情形 | 报告 |
|---|---|
| verify --change GREEN/GAPS，delta 含 MODIFIED（或 rename-then-modify） | JSON `modifiedIntegrity` **恒存在**（无差异条目也出现，见 B4）；human 段按 B4 显著性规则 |
| verify --change 任一 ERROR 类 | 字段与段落一律缺省 |
| verify --specs | 永不出现（byte golden continues） |
| archive dry-run / --write preflight 全部通过 | human 段输出 |
| archive 任一 preflight/合并失败（malformed、missing target、CAS、denial、conflict、hygiene） | 无报告段（失败信息照旧）；部分模块失败时成功模块也不输出（fail 即整体拒绝——archive 现有原子语义） |
| idempotent rerun（unchanged 路径） | 该 MODIFIED 无差异——JSON 条目 diff 全空，human 静默 |

### B4. 输出契约（REQ-3 收口）

- **JSON（verify --change，GREEN/GAPS 恒在）**：`modifiedIntegrity: [ { file, name, retained: [{id, title}], titleChanged: [{id, oldTitle, newTitle}], dropped: [{id, title}], added: [{id, title}], ambiguous: [{key, side: 'old'|'new'|'both', oldCount, newCount}], missingLines: [{scenario: <id|title|null>, line: <完整原文，不截断> }] } ]`。取值裁定（REQ-3 r2）：`file` = store 相对 suffix（与 projection.modules 同制）；`retained.title` 取**新块**原始标题（规范化仅用于配对判定）；`ambiguous.key` = ID 键取 ID，标题键取规范化标题；每个 MODIFIED 操作恒一条（含全空 diff 条目）；排序：条目按 (file,name)；retained/titleChanged/dropped 按旧块出现序，added 按新块出现序；**ambiguous**：o>0 的键按旧块首次出现序在前，o=0 的 new-only 键按新块首次出现序追加其后；missingLines 按旧块行序。无 ID 场景 id=null 以 title 表达。
- **human（verify 与 archive 同一段式）**：段标题 `— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —`；**显著性规则（Q1/REQ-3 r2 终裁）**：某 MODIFIED 条目存在 dropped/missingLines/ambiguous/titleChanged 任一非空即打印该条目——dropped/missingLines/ambiguous 行冠 `!` 前缀，titleChanged 一行提示（无 `!`），retained/added 只出计数；**全部条目四类皆空时整段静默**。行截断沿用 state A 惯例：超过 120 时 `slice(0,119)+'…'`（总长恒 ≤120，JS UTF-16 code unit 计）；JSON 保留全文。archive 的 warning（B2 降级路径）：**整行**先拼 `warning: modified-integrity <reason>` 再整串过 state-A `sanitizeMsg`（控制字符替换为 `·`，非删除；整行 ≤200 UTF-16 code units 含省略号），写 stderr。
- **确认机制（REQ-9 收口）**：**不新增**任何确认/override flag——含 dropped 的 `--write` 与既有流程完全相同；Q2 删除。

## 范围外（won't do）

req-v1 各项保持；另明确：single-file archive form；`--json` for archive；跨块移动检测（dropped+added 如实）；requirement 块的新旧散文"新增行"不报（只报旧内容缺失——保真检查的方向性）。

## 验收标准（确定 oracle）

- AC1（场景级互斥分类）：旧块 KV-01/KV-02/KV-03，新块 KV-01(同)/KV-03(标题变)/KV-04 → retained=[KV-01]、titleChanged=[KV-03(old/new 标题)]、dropped=[KV-02]、added=[KV-04]。
- AC2（行级）：KV-01 旧体 4 行、新体缺一条 AND → missingLines 含该行全文；行尾空白/行首缩进/tab 差异不报；重排的行如实报缺；重复行次数不足按次报缺；fence 内行参与比较；requirement 散文缺行报 scenario:null。
- AC3（ambiguous 与排序）：旧块内 KV-05 出现两次 → ambiguous 含 {key:KV-05, side:'old', oldCount:2, newCount:0..} 且不进其他四类；混合案例——old-present 键与两个 new-only 键并存 → 数组顺序为旧块首次出现序 + 新块首次出现序追加（deepStrictEqual）；基数真值表八行**表驱动**逐行断言（r3 advisory 吸收）。
- AC4（verdict/exit 确定 oracle，REQ-11 收口）：AC1 fixture 配 TAP（覆盖新块全部场景且全绿、无孤儿）→ verify --change **恰为 GREEN exit 0**（报告在场不改判）；同 fixture 去掉一条测试 → **GAPS exit 1**；archive dry-run **exit 0** 且 RESULT 行与现版本逐字一致、--write 写入字节与现版本一致；gate --change 输出与现版本一致（不消费报告）。
- AC5（json schema）：AC1 fixture 的 modifiedIntegrity 整条 deepStrictEqual（含排序）；无差异 MODIFIED 条目 diff 全空但存在；一切 verify ERROR 类字段缺省；--specs 永不出现（golden 复核）。
- AC6（执行纪律与 archive id-pattern 全路径，REQ-4 r2 收口）：verify config-origin 下 matcher batch 计数仍恰好 2（旧块标题在第一批 payload 断言）；invalid pattern 0 投影 0 spawn 无报告。archive 面五路径：①自定义 config pattern 正向——报告正确识别 default 认不出的 ID（如 AC-08a）；②无 config 时 default 正向；③invalid config row（matcher 构造前失败）→ warning+无报告段+archive 结果照旧；④matcher timeout/failure 类（注入）→ 同 ③；⑤warning 断言：完整字符串 oracle——stderr 行 = sanitizeMsg('warning: modified-integrity ' + reason)，长度 ≤200，控制字符已替换为 ·。
- AC7（活教材，冻结 fixture）：verify-change-scope 的 delta MODIFIED 块与其 store 原块**复制为测试 fixture**（内容冻结）→ 报告 deepStrictEqual 于**人工独立推导**的期望值（先读 fixture 手工列出 retained 7/titleChanged 2（SR-16、SR-18 含 old/new 标题）/dropped 0/added 0/ambiguous 0/missingLines 逐行清单，写死于测试——绝不以实现输出回填）。
- AC8（rename-then-modify 与结构边界）：RENAMED A->G + MODIFIED G（散文与场景逐字保留）→ 条目 name=G 且 **missingLines 为空**；闭合 fence 内伪场景标题不切分场景、其行参与体比较；**未闭合 fence 后的场景标题照常切分**（与 verify 采集一致断言）；`####  Scenario:`（多空格）等 state-A 正则可接受形式按有效标题处理。
- AC9（archive 面）：同 AC1 fixture 走 archive dry-run → human 段与 verify 同数据；preflight 失败（CAS mismatch）→ 无报告段；--write 成功时报告在写入前打印。
- AC10（兼容与文档）：--specs byte golden 绿；存量测试（本轮基线 306）全绿；docs/cli 双语（verify/archive 节）+ RUNBOOK 双语 MODIFIED 警告句旁补一句机械报告 + CHANGELOG；check --self 绿。

## 需求裁定记录（对 r3 评审）

- REQ-3：ambiguous 排序规则（old-present 旧序在前 + new-only 新序追加）；warning 整行组装后过 sanitizeMsg（· 替换、整行 ≤200）；AC3 混合排序案例 + AC6 全串 oracle。
- REQ-10：⑥ 修正为 state A 实况（未闭合开栏为普通文本、后续标题照常切分）；④ 改为 state-A 实际正则；AC8 补未闭合 fence 与多空格标题两案例。

# TRUTH — archive-merge (`lib/archive-merge.js`, CLI subcommand `archive`)

## Contract (code-is-truth)

source-commit: bfadb12

**Responsibility.** Merge a change's delta spec into the living store by stable Requirement ID (STEP6 archive action); move the change dir to `changes/archive/`. Zero deps. 3.1: also the home of the merge-family pure helpers shared with `verify --change` — delta discovery, projection building, CAS base stamps, realpath containment — plus the high-level `archive --change` transaction and the `stamp` subcommand.

**Delta format.** `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED Requirements` (h2 sections) containing `### Requirement: <name>` blocks (h3); RENAMED carries `- Old -> New` lines; optionally ONE CAS stamp line `<!-- apriori-base: sha256:<64hex> | new -->` before the first section. Parsing: `parseDeltaStrict(text)` → `{ delta, stamp, problems[] }` — since delta-consumption (3.3.x) a sequential fully-consuming line walker: every line outside opaque ``` fences belongs to exactly one legal construct (file preamble, legal section heading, section-preamble free text, requirement block, RENAMED rename line), and anything else — unrecognized h2 headings (one problem per heading, its lines attributed to NOTHING, stamps included), requirement/scenario markers outside their legal home, duplicate requirement names (within/across sections), and stamp problems (malformed/duplicate/late, matched in every non-skipped state) — is a `problems[]` entry carrying its 1-based line number; CRLF parses identically to LF; well-formed deltas parse byte-identically to the pre-walker grammar; `parseDelta(text)` = its delta only; `parseRequirementsStrict(text)` → `{ map, duplicates[] }` (first block wins, duplicates reported); `parseRequirements` = its map; `deltaOpCount(delta)` → total parsed ops (0 = no recognized section OR recognized sections with no parseable requirement/rename lines). `parseStamp(text)` → `{ stamp, problems }` — any standalone comment line ATTEMPTING apriori-base that fails the strict form is a problem, never silently 'unstamped'. `fingerprint(text)` → `sha256:<64hex>` over CRLF/CR→LF-normalized content.

**Public interface.**
- `merge(storeText, delta, change)` — PURE. → `{ store:Map, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged }`. Duplicate requirement names in the STORE text → conflicts (corruption guard). Order of application: RENAMED first (in-place key swap preserving document order), then ADDED, MODIFIED, REMOVED.
  - ADDED on existing name: identical trimmed content → `unchanged` (idempotent rerun no-op) UNLESS the name was created by this run's RENAMED (`renamedTargets`) → conflict.
  - MODIFIED replaces the whole block; missing target → conflict; delta block trim-equal to the store block → `unchanged` (the rerun/idempotence signature, since cas-enforcement).
  - REMOVED keeps the block, injects `_deprecated (superseded by <change>)_` into the heading line.
  - RENAMED with source gone + target present → `unchanged` ("already renamed" rerun signature); source gone + target absent → conflict; target already exists → conflict.
  - REMOVED with target gone but `<name>  _deprecated (superseded by <THIS change>)_` present → `unchanged` ("already deprecated" rerun signature); deprecated by a DIFFERENT change → conflict.
  - ANY conflict → nothing written (CLI exit 1).
- `renderStore(storeText, store)` — preserves the store's header (text before the first `### Requirement:`), then blocks joined with `\n`.
- `archiveStamp(d)` → `YYYY-MM-DDThhmm` (colon-free, CLI clock).
- `CHANGE_NAME_RE` = `/^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/` (same rule as `apriori new`).
- `archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops?)` — validates name; realpath containment on BOTH source and destination (a symlinked `archive/` pointing outside is rejected before any move); `renameSync` (via ops when given) to `changes/archive/<stamp>-<name>/`; returns dest or null when source missing; throws on invalid name/escape.
- `containsReal(root, target)` — realpath containment; not-yet-existing targets judged by nearest existing ancestor; STRICT (root itself is not inside root); fail-closed on unreadable paths.
- `discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name)` → `{ files:[{abs,suffix}], errors[] }` — validates name (CHANGE_NAME_RE), change-dir existence + containment, specs/ itself containment-checked before becoming a root (symlinked specs/ rejected); walk follows symlink dirents explicitly (escaping links = errors, never silent skips). Errors are VALIDATION class → exit 2 on both surfaces.
- `buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change)` → `{ texts:Map<suffix,text>, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired }` — all store files pass through; each delta strict-parsed, CAS-checked (mismatch+all-ops-unchanged → repaired/noted instead of failed), merged in memory; nothing touches disk.
- `archiveChange({cwd, change, changesDir, changesDirExplicit, write, ops})` → `{ code, out[], err[] }` — four phases, NO staging phase and NO post-commit writes of any kind (change-bundle, 4.0.0): the change dir is a complete bundle (requirement/ history, review/ evidence, gap-report.md, specs/ already inside), so the single atomic move carries everything; the command reads and writes nothing under any legacy root, is track-agnostic, and never deletes `spike/` (executor protocol, pre-archive). The four phases: preflight (discovery+hygiene+CAS+merges+pre-existing-temp guard+destination containment; any failure → nothing written), stage (`<store>.tmp-archive`, this-run cleanup on failure), commit (sorted per-file atomic renames; mid-commit failure → exact committed/not-committed/temps report, NO rollback), move (only changesDirExplicit, only after all commits). `ops` ({writeFileSync,renameSync,rmSync}) is the fault-injection seam.
- `stampCli(argv)` — exactly one argv entry; absent file → `new` form; directory/unreadable → exit 2.
- `cli(argv)`: high-level form `--change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]` (no --store/--delta) → archiveChange; single-file form `--store <FILE> --delta <FILE> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]` unchanged from 3.0.1 EXCEPT it now checks a present CAS stamp (mismatch → exit 1, nothing written) and rejects malformed deltas via parseDeltaStrict problems. Mixing --change with only one of --store/--delta → exit 2. Exit: 0 merged/no-op · 1 conflict/CAS/hygiene/zero-op/stage/commit/move-failure/pre-existing-temp · 2 usage/invalid name/missing change dir/containment.

**Transactional write ordering (`--write`).** store staged to `<store>.tmp-archive` → dir move (atomic rename) → store committed via `renameSync(tmp, store)`. Move failure → tmp discarded, store byte-for-byte untouched. Limits: (a) tmp+rename gives single-file replace atomicity, NOT crash durability (no fsync on file or dir) — do not claim crash-durable; (b) the final `renameSync(tmp, store)` is NOT guarded — if it throws AFTER the change dir moved, the store is unchanged but the change dir is already archived and the tmp file is left behind (the "all or nothing" claim covers move failure, not store-commit failure after the move).

**Three moments.** The store file is the shared state: init = created on first `--write`; update = full rewrite via temp+rename; cleanup = none (grows monotonically; REMOVED deprecates, never deletes).

**Pitfalls.**
- `merge` compares ADDED blocks with `.trim()` equality — whitespace-only differences count as conflicts, not no-ops.
- `parseRequirementsStrict` keeps the FIRST duplicate block (previously last-wins) — visible only on invalid input, which now conflicts anyway.
- `renderStore` drops anything BETWEEN requirement blocks that isn't part of a block (inter-block prose is not preserved — only the pre-first-block header is). Needs human confirmation whether any store relies on inter-block prose (repo's own stores do not).
- CAS enforcement is GRADED since cas-enforcement (3.3.x): an unstamped delta STILL MERGES, but mutation-carrying ones (MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED — ADDED-only exempt, the exemption is clobber-focused) warn on every surface (`unstampedWarning(suffix)`, both archive forms + verify --change stderr) and `buildProjection` returns `unstampedMutations: string[]` (suffixes) that gate C7 denies by default (--no-cas / `| cas | optional |` waive it, visibly). Stamps become mandatory in 4.0. Rerun repair: a stamped file whose base mismatches but whose EVERY op is `unchanged` (the already-applied signature; MODIFIED now reports `unchanged` on trim-equality) downgrades to a `rerun accepted` note in `notes[]`/`repaired[]` and contributes no writes — the move phase completes; a mismatch with ANY real pending op still fails the whole preflight.
- Deprecation rewrites the heading LINE (`### Requirement: Name  _deprecated (superseded by x)_`), so on re-parse the requirement's map KEY includes the marker — MODIFIED/RENAMED can no longer address the block by its original name. (REMOVED reruns by the SAME change are now a no-op signature; see merge().) `DEPRECATED_RE`/`stripDeprecatedBlocks` exported — spec-runner uses them to exclude deprecated blocks from scenario collection.
- CLI parsing via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md): --help exits 0; unknown flags/stray positionals exit 2; stampCli takes exactly one positional (a dash token is an unknown flag now, not the file).

## Decisions (doc-is-truth)

- D-AM-1 (active): same-ID conflict = stop, ledger issue, human resolves — the §4.11 serialize-per-module rule; the tool never auto-resolves.
- D-AM-2 (superseded-by: D-AM-7): archive rerun idempotency as ratified in 3.0.1 covered two signatures — identical-content ADDED, and RENAMED source-gone+target-present.
- D-AM-3 (active): a content-bearing delta parsing to zero operations errors instead of silently merging nothing.
- D-AM-4 (active): REMOVED deprecates in place (audit trail), never physically deletes.
- D-AM-5 (active): CAS base stamps are opt-in — §4.11's serialize rule tooling must not break unstamped pre-3.1 deltas. Ratified in change-projection (2026-07-10).
- D-AM-6 (active): high-level archive guarantees failure-atomicity UP TO THE COMMIT POINT only; the commit phase is per-file atomic renames with an honest report on mid-commit failure and NO rollback (a rollback that half-fails is worse); crash durability (fsync) is explicitly NOT claimed (§4.8 wording discipline). Ratified in change-projection.
- D-AM-7 (superseded-by: D-AM-8): rerun idempotency covered THREE signatures — identical-content ADDED; RENAMED source-gone+target-present; REMOVED already-deprecated-by-THIS-change. MODIFIED reruns re-applied. Supersedes D-AM-2. Ratified in change-projection.
- D-AM-8 (active): rerun idempotency covers FOUR signatures — the D-AM-7 three plus MODIFIED trim-equality → `unchanged`. This is what makes the CAS rerun repair sound: a stamped delta whose base mismatches but whose every op hits a signature is already fully applied and re-runs cleanly (repaired suffixes contribute no write jobs, so they need no temp-ownership handling). Supersedes D-AM-7. Ratified in cas-enforcement.
- D-AM-10 (active): unstamped mutation deltas are DENIED by default on both archive forms (preflight set, nothing written); waivers are visible and named (--no-cas wins over | cas | optional |). verify --change stays warn-only. Ratified in cas-mandatory (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 review P0-2).
- D-AM-9 (active): the atomic move carries the whole bundle — no staging phase, no post-commit writes; the 3.4.x requirement-staging phase (req-sweep) is deleted because the bundle already contains its history. `spike/` deletion is executor protocol pre-archive; the command never touches it. Ratified in change-bundle (2026-07-12).
# TRUTH — spec-runner (`lib/spec-runner.js`, CLI subcommand `verify`)

## Contract (code-is-truth)

source-commit: d7d65d3

**Responsibility.** Bind spec scenario IDs to a TAP-emitting test run; report per-scenario red/green; act as STEP5's deterministic gate. Fail-closed: a broken or vacuous run is never GREEN. 3.1: `--change <name>` verifies against the in-memory PROJECTED store (archive's merge() applied to the living store) — the mid-change form.

**Public interface (module exports).**
- `leadId(text, idRe)` → leading ID or null. Word-boundary strict: `XX-01b`, `XX-01_x` are NOT `XX-01`.
- `stripFences(text)` → text with ``` fenced blocks removed (fenced scenarios are documentation, not spec).
- `mdFiles` is internal; `collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe)` → `{ byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets, duplicates:[{id,files}] }`. A target may be a dir (recursed for `*.md`, sorted) or a single file (accepted as-is, ANY extension); dedupes overlapping targets. Scenario discovery matches exact h4 lines `#### Scenario: …` only. Scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks (heading carries `_deprecated (superseded by …)_`) are excluded in EVERY form — not demanded, not listed; their lingering tests turn ORPHAN.
- `collectScenariosFromTexts(Map<label,text>, idRe)` — same scan over in-memory texts (the projection path); labels are store-relative suffixes.
- `parseTap(out, idRe)` → `{ results: Map<id,{pass,fail,skip}>, untagged:[], untaggedFails, bailout, plans:number[], points, dupNumbers:number[] }`. Only lines shaped exactly `ok|not ok <n> - <name>` BIND — a TAP result line without the ` - ` separator produces no binding. Since tap-plan (3.3.x) a per-line pre-pass (one trailing `\r` stripped) additionally collects: `plans` = every top-level `1..N` (trailing `# SKIP`/`# TODO` directive legal, N still parses); `points` = count of top-level result tokens `/^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/` (bare/unnumbered points count; `ok:`-prefixed and indented lines never do); `dupNumbers` = complete numeric point tokens (`ok 1abc` stays unnumbered) seen more than once, numerically. `# SKIP`/`# TODO` counts as skip (proves nothing); untagged `not ok` without directive increments `untaggedFails`; `Bail out!` line captured.
- `zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged)` → true when output exists but no TAP line parsed AND no `TAP version`/`1..N` plan line (plan line = TAP reporter with an empty suite, not a reporter problem).
- `runTestCommand(cmd, cwd)` → `{ out (stdout+\n+stderr), status (null if signal-killed), signal, error }` via `spawnSync(shell:true)`.
- `evaluate(byId, unidentified, results)` → `{ boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, clean }`. A scenario whose only results are skips is UNBOUND.
- `verify(opts {specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd})` — with `change`: builds the projection via archive-merge's discoverDeltas/buildProjection (roots `<cwd>/apriori/{changes,specs}`); ANY projection failure (validation/hygiene/CAS/merge-conflict) → run with `errors` set and NO test executed; success → normal pipeline over projected texts; run gains `projection: {change, modules, conflicts}`. Without `change`: unchanged. → run object `{ verdict, results, fileCount, failCount, scenarioCount, missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, noTap, exec{status,signal,error}, errors }`. `failCount` = tagged fails + `untaggedFails`.
- `infraErrors(run)` → string[] (empty = trustworthy). Triggers: missing spec target · zero spec files · zero scenarios · spawn error · signal kill · bailout · zero-TAP-parsed · plan trio (defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes valid: multiple top-level plans → error naming the cure; exactly one plan and plan ≠ points → truncated/garbled; exactly one plan and duplicate point numbers) · non-zero exit with `failCount === 0` (TAP picture doesn't explain the exit). Gate surfaces any of these as ERROR exit 2 (`verify: <msg>`), outranking a C1 binding block; doctor's probe classification intentionally does not consume infraErrors.
- `formatReport(...)` → human report; RESULT line: `ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)` / `GREEN — spec is the test suite` / `GAPS`. Duplicates block GREEN.
- `verifyJson(run)` → `{ clean, result: GREEN|GAPS|ERROR, errors, specFiles, exec, duplicates, boundGreen[], boundRed[], unbound[], orphan[], unidentified[] }`; `--change` runs add exactly one field `projection: {change, modules, conflicts}` — never present otherwise. Since cas-enforcement (3.3.x): `--change` runs carry `projection.unstampedMutations` (suffixes, `[]` when none) + `projection.notes`; the cli prints one stderr warning per unstamped mutation delta — verdict untouched (WARN grade).
- `configTestCmd(cwd)` → test-cmd from `apriori/process-config.md`'s `| test-cmd | … |` row, or null.
- `cli(argv)` → exit code via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md): --help exits 0; unknown flags/stray positionals exit 2. `--specs <dir...>` OR `--change <name>` (mutually exclusive) `--test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern] [--cwd] [--json]`; test-cmd falls back to config row. Exit: 0 GREEN · 1 gaps/duplicates · 2 infra/usage error, incl. all projection failure classes.
- `DEFAULT_ID` = `[A-Z]+-\d+`.

**Data flow.** spec targets → collectScenarios → run test cmd once → parseTap → evaluate → infraErrors → report/JSON. One test-command invocation per verify run; no state persists between runs (stateless, reads FS + spawns only).

**Three moments.** No external shared state. FS reads only; the spawned test command is the only side effect.

**Pitfalls.**
- `--specs` consumes following args until the next `--` flag — a path starting with `--` is impossible.
- A direct-file `--specs` target is read whatever its extension; only directory recursion filters to `.md`.
- `exec.status` is null when signal-killed; the non-zero-exit rule explicitly guards `status !== null`.
- Requires `./archive-merge` (one-way: archive-merge never requires spec-runner).

## Decisions (doc-is-truth)

- D-SR-1 (active): fail-closed is a hard product stance — exit taxonomy 0/1/2; a vacuous or untrustworthy run must never exit 0. Ratified with 3.0.1 (owner + external review 2026-07-10).
- D-SR-2 (active): skipped tests prove nothing — a scenario with only skips is UNBOUND, not green.
- D-SR-3 (active): duplicate scenario IDs are a binding ambiguity → GAPS (exit 1), not a warning.
- D-SR-4 (active): the binding gate speaks TAP only; non-TAP layers (e.g. Playwright E2E) sit above the gate as additional exit conditions, never replace it.
- D-SR-5 (active): deprecated requirement blocks are excluded from scenario collection in ALL verify forms — post-REMOVED stores must not demand tests for removed behavior forever; lingering tests surface as ORPHAN. Ratified in change-projection (2026-07-10, STEP0 REQ-2).
- D-SR-6 (active): mid-change, the STEP5 gate is `verify --change` (projection); plain `--specs` verify is the post-archive/store-only form. Ratified in change-projection.
- D-SR-x (active): any top-level non-directive `not ok` blocks GREEN whatever its shape — unattributed failures are GAPS (exit 1) even on exit 0, infra ERRORs keep precedence, and a non-zero exec status never exits 0. Ratified in unattributed-fail (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 review P0-1).
- D-SR-y (active): TAP is parsed by a version-aware line lexer over stdout only — closed version matrix {12,13,14,absent}, escape-aware directive splitting, opaque fail-closed YAML blocks, pragma-only ignoring; stderr is a reported diagnostics channel, never parsed. Ratified in tap-contract (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 fourth review).

# req-final — modified-block-integrity：MODIFIED 整块替换的保真性成为机械报告

target lineage: main（v4 产品线；不合并 v1/v3——两条 legacy 产品线分支）。**state A 声明（REQ-8）**：本 change 构建在工作区中两个未归档先行 change（gate-id-pattern：id-pattern 解析与可终止 matcher；verify-change-scope：change-scoped verdict、单标题批、deltaOps、storeReport）之上；三者按序归档，spec/design 以工作区代码为 state A。

## 背景（现状 A）

同 req-v1（复盘 P0-3 唯一 ⚠ 防线项；活教材 = verify-change-scope delta 的 9 场景块重述；merge() 整块替换零对比；对比原料在 buildProjection 处零成本在手）。

## 目标（目标态 B）

### B1. 保真报告的判定算法（REQ-1/REQ-2 收口）

**规范化**：行统一 CRLF/CR→LF；比较前每行去行尾空白、行首空白（含 tab）整体剥离；空行不参与比较。

**occurrence 级场景配对（唯一基数真值表，REQ-2 r2 收口）**：配对键 = 场景 ID（有 ID 时）或规范化全标题（无 ID 时），同一张表对两类键统一适用。对每个键统计旧块出现数 o、新块出现数 n：

| o | n | 分类 |
|---:|---:|---|
| 0 | 1 | added |
| 1 | 0 | dropped |
| 1 | 1 | 正常配对（有 ID 键：同规范化标题→retained，异→titleChanged，两类互斥且都做体行比较；标题键：即 retained） |
| 0 | >1 | ambiguous(side=new) |
| >1 | 0 | ambiguous(side=old) |
| >1 | 1 | ambiguous(side=old) |
| 1 | >1 | ambiguous(side=new) |
| >1 | >1 | ambiguous(side=both) |

ambiguous 的键**完全跳过** retained/titleChanged/dropped/added 与体行比较；每键一条 entry，携带 side 与两侧计数。

**行级比较（顺序保持子序列，唯一算法）**：对每个 retained/titleChanged 场景，取旧场景体的规范化非空行序列 O 与新体序列 N，做**保序贪心子序列匹配**（O 的每行按序在 N 中找首个未被消费的相等行；重复行按出现次数逐一消费）——未匹配的旧行即 `missingLines`（重排导致的失配如实报告；子序列不要求连续）。

**结构扫描规则（REQ-10 收口）**：① Requirement 标题行（`### Requirement: …`）**永不**参与比较（rename-then-modify 不得因标题差异假报缺失）；② requirement 散文 = 标题行之后第一行起、至第一个**有效**场景标题前，同规则比较，缺行记 `missingLines`（scenario=null，仅报旧内容缺失）；③ 场景体 = 有效场景标题之后至下一个有效场景标题或块尾；④ 有效场景标题沿用 state A 的**实际正则**（`/^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/m`——多空格/tab 均可），fence 感知与 `stripFences` 完全一致——仅**闭合的**三反引号对内的行不是分隔符；⑤ fence 定界行与围栏内非空行都作为当前散文/场景体的比较行；⑥ 未闭合 fence 沿用 state A 实况（`stripFences` 只剥闭合对）：未闭合的开栏行是普通文本，其后的场景标题**照常切分**——与 verify 的场景采集逐字一致（REQ-10 r3 修正：v3 的'吞尾'表述系对 state A 的误判，废止）。

### B2. 消费面与执行纪律（REQ-4/REQ-5 收口）

- **`verify --change`**：旧块标题作为第 4 类 tag 并入**既有的单一标题批**（不新增 matcher 调用——SR-63 的两批契约保持；这些 pairs 不进入绑定 acc，仅供 integrity 配对）；invalid pattern / matcher failure / projection failure 走既有 ERROR 路径，此时**无** integrity 报告。id-pattern 解析与 state A 完全一致（flag > config > default）。
- **`archive --change`（high-level form 专属；single-file form 明确 out of scope——一模块手术是专家手动路径）**：dry-run 与 `--write` preflight 输出同一数据语义的 human 段（**不新增 --json**，REQ-3 附带裁定）。id 提取沿用统一解析（config > default，archive 无 flag）与可终止通道——经 **bin 层依赖注入**（bin/apriori.js 同时持有两模块，把 spec-runner 的 matcher 工厂注入 archive cli 的可选参数；archive-merge 模块自身仍零依赖 spec-runner，方向不破）。invalid config pattern 或 matcher failure → 打印一条 bounded warning、**跳过 integrity 报告**，archive 的一切既有语义（exit/写入/输出其余部分）不变。
- **`gate`**：不消费。

### B3. 报告存在性矩阵（REQ-6 收口）

| 情形 | 报告 |
|---|---|
| verify --change GREEN/GAPS，delta 含 MODIFIED（或 rename-then-modify） | JSON `modifiedIntegrity` **恒存在**（无差异条目也出现，见 B4）；human 段按 B4 显著性规则 |
| verify --change 任一 ERROR 类 | 字段与段落一律缺省 |
| verify --specs | 永不出现（byte golden continues） |
| archive dry-run / --write preflight 全部通过 | human 段输出 |
| archive 任一 preflight/合并失败（malformed、missing target、CAS、denial、conflict、hygiene） | 无报告段（失败信息照旧）；部分模块失败时成功模块也不输出（fail 即整体拒绝——archive 现有原子语义） |
| idempotent rerun（unchanged 路径） | 该 MODIFIED 无差异——JSON 条目 diff 全空，human 静默 |

### B4. 输出契约（REQ-3 收口）

- **JSON（verify --change，GREEN/GAPS 恒在）**：`modifiedIntegrity: [ { file, name, retained: [{id, title}], titleChanged: [{id, oldTitle, newTitle}], dropped: [{id, title}], added: [{id, title}], ambiguous: [{key, side: 'old'|'new'|'both', oldCount, newCount}], missingLines: [{scenario: <id|title|null>, line: <完整原文，不截断> }] } ]`。取值裁定（REQ-3 r2）：`file` = store 相对 suffix（与 projection.modules 同制）；`retained.title` 取**新块**原始标题（规范化仅用于配对判定）；`ambiguous.key` = ID 键取 ID，标题键取规范化标题；每个 MODIFIED 操作恒一条（含全空 diff 条目）；排序：条目按 (file,name)；retained/titleChanged/dropped 按旧块出现序，added 按新块出现序；**ambiguous**：o>0 的键按旧块首次出现序在前，o=0 的 new-only 键按新块首次出现序追加其后；missingLines 按旧块行序。无 ID 场景 id=null 以 title 表达。
- **human（verify 与 archive 同一段式）**：段标题 `— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —`；**显著性规则（Q1/REQ-3 r2 终裁）**：某 MODIFIED 条目存在 dropped/missingLines/ambiguous/titleChanged 任一非空即打印该条目——dropped/missingLines/ambiguous 行冠 `!` 前缀，titleChanged 一行提示（无 `!`），retained/added 只出计数；**全部条目四类皆空时整段静默**。行截断沿用 state A 惯例：超过 120 时 `slice(0,119)+'…'`（总长恒 ≤120，JS UTF-16 code unit 计）；JSON 保留全文。archive 的 warning（B2 降级路径）：**整行**先拼 `warning: modified-integrity <reason>` 再整串过 state-A `sanitizeMsg`（控制字符替换为 `·`，非删除；整行 ≤200 UTF-16 code units 含省略号），写 stderr。
- **确认机制（REQ-9 收口）**：**不新增**任何确认/override flag——含 dropped 的 `--write` 与既有流程完全相同；Q2 删除。

## 范围外（won't do）

req-v1 各项保持；另明确：single-file archive form；`--json` for archive；跨块移动检测（dropped+added 如实）；requirement 块的新旧散文"新增行"不报（只报旧内容缺失——保真检查的方向性）。

## 验收标准（确定 oracle）

- AC1（场景级互斥分类）：旧块 KV-01/KV-02/KV-03，新块 KV-01(同)/KV-03(标题变)/KV-04 → retained=[KV-01]、titleChanged=[KV-03(old/new 标题)]、dropped=[KV-02]、added=[KV-04]。
- AC2（行级）：KV-01 旧体 4 行、新体缺一条 AND → missingLines 含该行全文；行尾空白/行首缩进/tab 差异不报；重排的行如实报缺；重复行次数不足按次报缺；fence 内行参与比较；requirement 散文缺行报 scenario:null。
- AC3（ambiguous 与排序）：旧块内 KV-05 出现两次 → ambiguous 含 {key:KV-05, side:'old', oldCount:2, newCount:0..} 且不进其他四类；混合案例——old-present 键与两个 new-only 键并存 → 数组顺序为旧块首次出现序 + 新块首次出现序追加（deepStrictEqual）；基数真值表八行**表驱动**逐行断言（r3 advisory 吸收）。
- AC4（verdict/exit 确定 oracle，REQ-11 收口）：AC1 fixture 配 TAP（覆盖新块全部场景且全绿、无孤儿）→ verify --change **恰为 GREEN exit 0**（报告在场不改判）；同 fixture 去掉一条测试 → **GAPS exit 1**；archive dry-run **exit 0** 且 RESULT 行与现版本逐字一致、--write 写入字节与现版本一致；gate --change 输出与现版本一致（不消费报告）。
- AC5（json schema）：AC1 fixture 的 modifiedIntegrity 整条 deepStrictEqual（含排序）；无差异 MODIFIED 条目 diff 全空但存在；一切 verify ERROR 类字段缺省；--specs 永不出现（golden 复核）。
- AC6（执行纪律与 archive id-pattern 全路径，REQ-4 r2 收口）：verify config-origin 下 matcher batch 计数仍恰好 2（旧块标题在第一批 payload 断言）；invalid pattern 0 投影 0 spawn 无报告。archive 面五路径：①自定义 config pattern 正向——报告正确识别 default 认不出的 ID（如 AC-08a）；②无 config 时 default 正向；③invalid config row（matcher 构造前失败）→ warning+无报告段+archive 结果照旧；④matcher timeout/failure 类（注入）→ 同 ③；⑤warning 断言：完整字符串 oracle——stderr 行 = sanitizeMsg('warning: modified-integrity ' + reason)，长度 ≤200，控制字符已替换为 ·。
- AC7（活教材，冻结 fixture）：verify-change-scope 的 delta MODIFIED 块与其 store 原块**复制为测试 fixture**（内容冻结）→ 报告 deepStrictEqual 于**人工独立推导**的期望值（先读 fixture 手工列出 retained 7/titleChanged 2（SR-16、SR-18 含 old/new 标题）/dropped 0/added 0/ambiguous 0/missingLines 逐行清单，写死于测试——绝不以实现输出回填）。
- AC8（rename-then-modify 与结构边界）：RENAMED A->G + MODIFIED G（散文与场景逐字保留）→ 条目 name=G 且 **missingLines 为空**；闭合 fence 内伪场景标题不切分场景、其行参与体比较；**未闭合 fence 后的场景标题照常切分**（与 verify 采集一致断言）；`####  Scenario:`（多空格）等 state-A 正则可接受形式按有效标题处理。
- AC9（archive 面）：同 AC1 fixture 走 archive dry-run → human 段与 verify 同数据；preflight 失败（CAS mismatch）→ 无报告段；--write 成功时报告在写入前打印。
- AC10（兼容与文档）：--specs byte golden 绿；存量测试（本轮基线 306）全绿；docs/cli 双语（verify/archive 节）+ RUNBOOK 双语 MODIFIED 警告句旁补一句机械报告 + CHANGELOG；check --self 绿。
