     3	## ADDED Requirements
     4	
     5	### Requirement: the modified-block integrity engine reports replacement fidelity
     6	A pure shared helper SHALL compare a MODIFIED operation's old store block against its replacement (both captured at the projection's own parse snapshot — `buildProjection` additionally returns the raw old/new block text pairs per MODIFIED operation; no consumer re-reads files) and report structural fidelity, WITHOUT changing any verdict, exit code or write semantics. Structure scan: the requirement heading line never participates; requirement prose runs from the line after the heading to the first VALID scenario heading (state A's actual regex `/^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/m`, fence-aware exactly like `stripFences` — only lines inside CLOSED triple-backtick pairs are not delimiters; an unclosed opener is ordinary text and later headings still split); fence delimiter lines and fenced non-empty lines are compared as body lines. Pairing runs per key (scenario ID when present, normalized full title otherwise) through one cardinality truth table — o=0,n=1 added; o=1,n=0 dropped; o=1,n=1 paired (same normalized title → retained, different → titleChanged, mutually exclusive, both body-compared); any side >1 → ambiguous (side old/new/both with counts; the key skips every other class and body comparison). Line comparison is ONE algorithm: order-preserving greedy subsequence over normalized non-empty lines (CRLF/CR→LF, trailing whitespace stripped, leading whitespace stripped; duplicates consumed by count); unmatched OLD lines are `missingLines` (requirement prose misses carry scenario=null; only old-content loss is reported). An idempotent replacement (identical trimmed text) yields an entry whose diff arrays are all empty.
     7	
     8	#### Scenario: AM-43 the cardinality truth table classifies every occurrence
     9	- WHEN an old block carries KV-01/KV-02/KV-03 and the replacement carries KV-01 (same title), KV-03 (changed title), KV-04 — and separately each of the eight o/n cardinality rows is exercised (including a duplicated KV-05 on one side and no-ID title keys)
    10	- THEN retained=[KV-01], titleChanged=[KV-03 with old/new titles], dropped=[KV-02], added=[KV-04]; each truth-table row lands in its one class; ambiguous entries carry {key, side, oldCount, newCount} and skip every other class; ambiguous ordering is old-present keys by old first occurrence, then new-only keys by new first occurrence
    11	
    12	#### Scenario: AM-44 the subsequence comparison reports lost lines faithfully
    13	- WHEN a retained scenario's replacement drops one AND clause, reorders two lines, repeats a line fewer times, differs only in indentation/trailing whitespace on another, and the requirement prose loses a sentence
    14	- THEN missingLines lists the dropped AND clause, the reordered-out line and the under-repeated line (by count) with full original text, the whitespace-only difference is NOT reported, and the prose loss carries scenario=null
    15	
    16	#### Scenario: AM-45 structure boundaries follow state A exactly
    17	- WHEN a rename-then-modify replacement preserves prose and scenarios verbatim under the new requirement name, a CLOSED fence contains a scenario-looking heading, an UNCLOSED fence precedes a real scenario heading, and a heading uses multiple spaces after `####`
    18	- THEN the rename case reports zero missingLines (the heading line is never compared); the fenced fake heading does not split a scenario and its lines are body-compared; the heading after the unclosed opener still splits (stripFences strips only closed pairs); the multi-space heading is a valid delimiter
    19	
    20	#### Scenario: AM-46 archive prints the integrity section without changing its semantics
    21	- WHEN `apriori archive --change <name>` (the high-level form only; the single-file form is out of scope) passes preflight with a MODIFIED delta whose replacement drops a scenario — on dry-run and on `--write`
    22	- THEN the same human integrity section prints (before any write on `--write`), the RESULT line, exit code and written bytes are identical to a run without the report, and any preflight failure (malformed, CAS, conflict, denial) prints NO integrity section
    23	
    24	#### Scenario: AM-47 the archive id-pattern channel is resolved, terminable and degradable
    25	- WHEN archive runs with a custom config `id-pattern` row recognizing letter-suffixed IDs; with no row (default); with an uncompilable row; and with an injected matcher failure
    26	- THEN the first two produce correct reports through the injected terminable matcher (bin wires it in; archive-merge itself never requires spec-runner); the last two print exactly one stderr warning — the WHOLE line `warning: modified-integrity <reason>` passed through sanitizeMsg (control chars replaced with `·`, ≤200 UTF-16 units) — skip the report, and leave every other archive output and exit unchanged

     5	### Requirement: verify --change carries the modified-block integrity report
     6	On a trustworthy `--change` run, `verify` SHALL attach the integrity engine's report for every MODIFIED operation (rename-then-modify targets included, keyed by the post-rename name with the pre-rename block as baseline). The old blocks' scenario titles ride the EXISTING single title batch as a fourth tag class that never enters binding accumulation, scenario counts or the change scope — the two-batch matcher contract stands. JSON: `modifiedIntegrity` is ALWAYS present on GREEN/GAPS (one entry per MODIFIED operation, empty-diff entries included; `file` = store-relative suffix; element shapes, value choices and orderings per the engine's contract; missingLines carry full untruncated text) and ABSENT on every ERROR class and on every `--specs` run (byte goldens continue to prove the latter). Human: a `— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —` section prints an entry when any of dropped/missingLines/ambiguous/titleChanged is non-empty (`!` prefix on dropped/missingLines/ambiguous lines; a plain one-line note per titleChanged; counts only for retained/added), stays entirely silent when every entry's four classes are empty, and truncates lines the state-A way (`slice(0,119)+'…'`). The report never changes the verdict or the exit code.

     8	#### Scenario: SR-65 the report rides the run without touching the verdict

    10	- THEN the run is still GREEN exit 0; `modifiedIntegrity` carries dropped=[KV-02] and the missing line with full text; the human section prints them with `!`; and removing one scoped test flips the same run to GAPS exit 1 with the identical report attached

    17	- WHEN a config-origin `--change` run with a MODIFIED delta executes with a counting child seam

    19

    21	- WHEN the engine runs over the frozen copy of the verify-change-scope change's real MODIFIED block (the nine-scenario `projected verify` requirement) against its frozen old store block
    22	- THEN the report deep-equals the hand-derived expectation: retained = the seven untouched scenarios, titleChanged = SR-16 and SR-18 (with their old/new titles), dropped=[], added=[], ambiguous=[], and missingLines exactly as hand-listed from the two rewritten scenario bodies
   340	function buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change) {
   341	  const texts = new Map(), perModule = new Map(), modules = [];
   342	  const conflicts = [], casMismatches = [], hygiene = [], validation = [];
   343	  const unstampedMutations = [], notes = [], repaired = [];
   344	  // per-suffix operation buckets captured at THIS parse — the one delta snapshot
   345	  // (change-scope provenance must never come from a second read of the file)
   346	  const deltaOps = new Map();
   347	  for (const abs of mdFilesUnder(storeRoot))
   348	    texts.set(path.relative(storeRoot, abs).split(path.sep).join('/'), fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8'));
   349	  for (const { abs, suffix } of deltaFiles) {
   350	    modules.push(suffix);
   351	    const target = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   352	    if (!containsReal(storeRoot, target)) { validation.push(`store target escapes ${storeRoot}: ${target}`); continue; }
   353	    const raw = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');
   354	    if (!raw.trim()) { hygiene.push(`empty delta file: ${abs}`); continue; }
   355	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(raw);
   356	    if (problems.length) { hygiene.push(...problems.map((p) => `${abs}: ${p}`)); continue; }
   357	    if (deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) { hygiene.push(`parsed 0 delta operations from ${abs} — it has content but no recognized operations`); continue; }
   358	    deltaOps.set(suffix, Object.freeze({
   359	      added: [...delta.ADDED.keys()], modified: [...delta.MODIFIED.keys()],
   360	      renamedPairs: delta.RENAMED.map((p) => [...p]), removed: [...delta.REMOVED.keys()],
   361	    }));
   362	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) unstampedMutations.push(suffix);   // WARN grade (callers print)
   363	    const storeText = texts.get(suffix) || '';
   364	    if (stamp) {
   365	      const actual = texts.has(suffix) ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   366	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   367	        // rerun repair: a mismatch whose every op is already applied is the resumed-rerun
   368	        // signature — divergence with ANY real pending op still fails exactly as before
   369	        const probe = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   370	        const pending = probe.merged.length + probe.modified.length + probe.deprecated.length + probe.renamed.length;
   371	        if (pending === 0 && probe.conflicts.length === 0) {
   372	          notes.push(`${suffix}: stamp mismatch but the delta is already fully applied — rerun accepted`);
   373	          perModule.set(suffix, probe);
   374	          repaired.push(suffix);
   375	          continue;                                          // contributes no writes
   376	        }
   377	        casMismatches.push(`base mismatch for ${target}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual}`); continue;
   378	      }
   379	    }
   380	    const r = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   381	    perModule.set(suffix, r);
   382	    if (r.conflicts.length) { conflicts.push(...r.conflicts.map((c) => `${suffix}: ${c}`)); continue; }
   383	    texts.set(suffix, renderStore(storeText, r.store));
   384	  }
   385	  modules.sort();
   386	  return { texts, modules, perModule, deltaOps, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired };
   387	}
   388	
   389	// ---- archive commit machinery ----------------------------------------------
   390	
   391	// colon-free date-time stamp YYYY-MM-DDThhmm (CLI's own clock; never an agent guess)
   392	function archiveStamp(d) {
   393	  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
   394	  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
   395	}
   396	
   397	const CHANGE_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;   // bare kebab-case — same rule as `apriori new`
   398	
   399	
   400	// Move an in-flight change dir (bare name) → changes/archive/<stamp>-<name>/
   401	// Fail-closed: source AND destination must resolve (realpath) strictly inside changesDir.
   402	function archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops) {
   403	  const rename = (ops && ops.renameSync) || fs.renameSync;
   404	  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(name)) throw new Error(`invalid change name '${name}' — bare kebab-case only`);
   405	  const src = path.join(changesDir, name);
   406	  if (!fs.existsSync(src)) return null;
   407	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, src)) throw new Error(`change path escapes ${changesDir}`);
   408	  const dest = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', `${archiveStamp(now)}-${name}`);
   409	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, dest)) throw new Error(`archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   410	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
   411	  rename(src, dest);
   412	  return dest;
   413	}
   414	
   415	const CATEGORY_LABELS = [['merged (ADDED)', 'merged'], ['modified (MODIFIED)', 'modified'],
   416	  ['deprecated (REMOVED)', 'deprecated'], ['renamed (RENAMED)', 'renamed'], ['already merged (no-op)', 'unchanged']];
   417	
   418	// High-level archive: the whole change, four phases (preflight → stage → commit → move).
   419	// → { code, out: [], err: [] } — pure return; cli() prints. ops is the fault-injection seam.
   420	function archiveChange(o) {
   421	  const out = [], err = [];
   422	  const ops = o.ops || { writeFileSync: fs.writeFileSync.bind(fs), renameSync: fs.renameSync.bind(fs), rmSync: fs.rmSync.bind(fs) };
   423	  const cwd = o.cwd || process.cwd();
   424	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, o.changesDir || path.join('apriori', 'changes'));
   425	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   426	
   427	  // --- phase 1: preflight (no writes) ---
   428	  const d = discoverDeltas(changesDir, o.change);
   429	  if (d.errors.length) { err.push(...d.errors.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   430	  const p = buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, o.change);
   431	  if (p.validation.length) { err.push(...p.validation.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   432	  // per-module report for whatever merged cleanly (dry-run info comes first, then failures)
   433	  for (const suffix of p.modules) {
   434	    const r = p.perModule.get(suffix);
   435	    if (!r || r.conflicts.length) continue;
   436	    out.push(`--- ${suffix}`);
   437	    for (const [label, key] of CATEGORY_LABELS) if (r[key].length) out.push(`${label}: ${r[key].join(', ')}`);
   438	  }
   439	  const casDenials = [];
   440	  if (p.unstampedMutations.length) {
   441	    const w = casWaiver(cwd, o.noCas);
   442	    if (w.problem) {
   443	      casDenials.push(`${w.problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)`);
   444	    } else if (!w.waiver) {
   445	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations)
   446	        casDenials.push(`${unstampedWarning(sfx)} — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row`);
   447	    } else {
   448	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations) out.push(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(sfx)}`);
   449	      out.push(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   450	    }
   451	  }
   452	  for (const n of p.notes) out.push(`note: ${n}`);
   453	  // CAS denial joins the preflight failure set — every diagnosis stays visible in one report
   454	  const preflightFailures = [...casDenials, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches, ...p.conflicts];
   455	  if (preflightFailures.length) {
   456	    for (const f of preflightFailures) err.push(`archive: ${f}`);
   457	    out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   458	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   459	  }
   460	  const repaired = new Set(p.repaired);                     // already applied — no writes for these
   461	  const jobs = d.files.filter(({ suffix }) => !repaired.has(suffix)).map(({ suffix }) => {
   462	    const store = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   463	    return { suffix, store, tmp: store + '.tmp-archive', text: p.texts.get(suffix) };
   464	  }).sort((a, b) => (a.store < b.store ? -1 : 1));
   465	  if (o.write) {
   466	    for (const j of jobs) if (fs.existsSync(j.tmp)) {
   467	      err.push(`archive: temp file already exists: ${j.tmp} — another run in flight or a manual-recovery artifact; not touching it`);
   468	      out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   469	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   470	    }
   471	    if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   472	      // destination containment is checked before any write, not at move time
   473	      const probe = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', 'probe');
   474	      if (!containsReal(changesDir, probe)) {
   475	        err.push(`archive: archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   476	        return { code: 2, out, err };
   477	      }
   478	    }
   479	  }
   480	  if (!o.write) {
   481	    out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${jobs.length} module(s)) — pass --write to apply`);
   482	    return { code: 0, out, err };
   483	  }
   484	
   485	  // --- phase 2: stage (this run's temps only — preflight proved none pre-existed) ---
   486	  const staged = [];
   487	  try {
   488	    for (const j of jobs) {
   489	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(j.store), { recursive: true });
   490	      ops.writeFileSync(j.tmp, j.text);
   491	      staged.push(j.tmp);
   492	    }
   493	  } catch (e) {
   494	    for (const t of staged) { try { ops.rmSync(t, { force: true }); } catch { /* best effort */ } }
   495	    err.push(`archive: staging failed (${e.message}) — stores untouched, this run's temp files removed`);
   496	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   497	  }
   498	
   499	  // --- phase 3: commit (per-file atomic renames, sorted order; no rollback on mid-commit failure) ---
   500	  const committed = [];
   501	  for (const j of jobs) {
   502	    try { ops.renameSync(j.tmp, j.store); committed.push(j); }
   503	    catch (e) {
   504	      err.push(`archive: COMMIT FAILED at ${j.suffix} (${e.message})`);
   505	      err.push(`  committed:     ${committed.map((c) => c.suffix).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
   506	      const remaining = jobs.filter((x) => !committed.includes(x));
   507	      err.push(`  NOT committed: ${remaining.map((x) => x.suffix).join(', ')}`);
   508	      err.push(`  temp files remaining for manual completion: ${remaining.map((x) => x.tmp).join(', ')}`);
   509	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   510	    }
   511	  }
   512	
   513	  // --- phase 4: move (only with explicit --changes-dir) ---
   514	  let moved = null;
   515	  if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   516	    try { moved = archiveChangeDir(changesDir, o.change, new Date(), ops); }
   517	    catch (e) { err.push(`archive: stores committed but the change-dir move failed: ${e.message} — rerun to complete`); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   518	    if (moved === null) { err.push('archive: stores committed but the change dir vanished before the move'); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   519	  }
   520	  out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${jobs.length} module store(s) rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   521	  return { code: 0, out, err };
   522	}
   523	
   524	// print the CAS stamp line for a store file's current content
   525	const STAMP_USAGE = 'usage: apriori stamp <store-file>';
   526	function stampCli(argv) {
   527	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'stamp', usage: STAMP_USAGE, positionals: 1, flags: {} }, (f, pos) => {
   528	    const p = pos[0];
   529	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) { console.log('<!-- apriori-base: new -->'); return 0; }
   530	    if (fs.statSync(p).isDirectory()) { console.error(`stamp: ${p} is a directory, not a store file`); return 2; }
   531	    let text;
   532	    try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
   533	    catch (e) { console.error(`stamp: cannot read ${p}: ${e.message}`); return 2; }
   534	    console.log(`<!-- apriori-base: ${fingerprint(text)} -->`);
   535	    return 0;
   536	  });
   537	}

   539	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]\n   or: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]';
   540	
   541	function cli(argv) {
   542	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'archive', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   543	    flags: { '--store': 'value', '--delta': 'value', '--change': 'value', '--write': 'flag', '--changes-dir': 'value', '--no-cas': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   544	    const a = { store: f['--store'] || null, delta: f['--delta'] || null, change: f['--change'] || null,
   545	      write: !!f['--write'], changesDir: f['--changes-dir'] || null, noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   546	  // high-level form: --change alone (no --store/--delta) drives the whole change
   547	    if (a.change && !a.store && !a.delta) {
   548	      const r = archiveChange({ cwd: process.cwd(), change: a.change, write: a.write,
   549	        changesDir: a.changesDir || undefined, changesDirExplicit: !!a.changesDir, noCas: a.noCas });
   550	      for (const l of r.out) console.log(l);
   551	      for (const l of r.err) console.error(l);
   552	      return r.code;
   553	    }
   554	    if (a.change && (a.store || a.delta) && !(a.store && a.delta)) {
   555	      console.error('usage: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (high-level form)\n   or: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (single-file form)\n(--change cannot be combined with only one of --store/--delta)');
   556	      return 2;
   557	    }
   558	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   559	    const vEarly = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(a.change);
   560	    if (!vEarly.ok) { console.error(`archive: invalid change name '${a.change}' (${vEarly.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`); return 2; }
   561	    if (a.changesDir && !fs.existsSync(path.join(a.changesDir, a.change))) {
   562	      console.error(`archive: change '${a.change}' not found under ${a.changesDir} — nothing to move`); return 2;
   563	    }
   564	    // first archive in a repo: the store file may not exist yet — start from an empty store
   565	    const storeExists = fs.existsSync(a.store);
   566	    const storeText = storeExists ? fs.readFileSync(a.store, 'utf8') : '';
   567	    if (!storeExists) console.log(`store ${a.store} does not exist yet — will be created${a.write ? '' : ' on --write'}`);
   568	    const deltaText = fs.readFileSync(a.delta, 'utf8');
   569	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(deltaText);
   570	    if (problems.length) {
   108	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
   109	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   110	}
   111	
   112	// One batch over tagged pairs ([label,title,scoped]) plus sibling titles (appended to the
   113	// SAME batch — never a third matcher call) → { acc, ids, siblingIds } or { failure }.
   114	function makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles = []) {
   115	  const titles = [...pairs.map((p) => p[1]), ...siblingTitles];
   116	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   117	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   118	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   119	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   120	    const id = res.ids[i];
   121	    if (id) {
   122	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   123	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   124	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   125	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   126	  });
   127	  const siblingIds = new Set(res.ids.slice(pairs.length).filter((x) => x !== null));
   128	  return { acc, ids: res.ids.slice(0, pairs.length), siblingIds };
   129	}
   130	
   131	// Scenario titles a SIBLING active change genuinely declares — an EXEMPTION source, so it
   132	// is strict: each sibling delta must parse cleanly (parseDeltaStrict, zero problems, >0 ops)
   690	  if (scopedIds.length === 0 && scopedUnidentified.length === 0)
   691	    run.vacuousNote = vacuousNoteFor(meta.deltaOps);
   692	}

   694	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   695	function verifyJson(run) {
   696	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   697	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   698	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   699	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   700	  if (run.storeReport) {                                  // ONLY non-ERROR --change runs (absent otherwise)
   701	    json.storeReport = { ...run.storeReport,
   702	      unidentified: run.storeReport.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })) };
   703	    json.changeScope = run.changeScope;
   704	  }
   705	  return json;
   706	}
   707	
   708	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   709	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   710	  return {
   711	    clean,
   712	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   713	    errors: run.errors,
   714	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   715	    exec: run.exec,
   716	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   717	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   718	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   719	    unbound: v.unbound,
   720	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   721	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   722	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   723	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   724	  };
   725	}

   727	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   728	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   729	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   730	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   731	  return value;
   732	}

   734	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   735	
   736	function cli(argv) {
   737	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   738	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   739	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   740	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   741	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   742	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   743	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   744	      return 2;
   745	    }
   746	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   747	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   748	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   749	      a.testCmd = c;
   750	    }
   751	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   752	    const run = verify(a);
   753	    if (run.projection)
   754	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   755	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   756	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   757	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   758	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr, storeReport: run.storeReport, vacuousNote: run.vacuousNote }));
   759	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   760	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   761	  });
   762	}

    37	    case undefined:
    38	    case '-h':
    39	    case '--help':  console.log(USAGE); return 0;
    40	    default:        console.error(`unknown command: ${sub}\n\n${USAGE}`); return 2;

An idempotent replacement (identical trimmed text) yields an entry whose diff arrays are all empty.

apriori/changes/modified-block-integrity/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:13:- WHEN a retained scenario's replacement drops one AND clause, reorders two lines, repeats a line fewer times, differs only in indentation/trailing whitespace on another, and the requirement prose loses a sentence
apriori/changes/modified-block-integrity/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:22:- THEN the same human integrity section prints (before any write on `--write`), the RESULT line, exit code and written bytes are identical to a run without the report, and any preflight failure (malformed, CAS, conflict, denial) prints NO integrity section

apriori/changes/modified-block-integrity/requirement/req-final.md:30:**行级比较（顺序保持子序列，唯一算法）**：对每个 retained/titleChanged 场景，取旧场景体的规范化非空行序列 O 与新体序列 N，做**保序贪心子序列匹配**（O 的每行按序在 N 中找首个未被消费的相等行；重复行按出现次数逐一消费）——未匹配的旧行即 `missingLines`（重排导致的失配如实报告；子序列不要求连续）。
apriori/changes/modified-block-integrity/requirement/req-final.md:49:| idempotent rerun（unchanged 路径） | 该 MODIFIED 无差异——JSON 条目 diff 全空，human 静默 |
apriori/changes/modified-block-integrity/requirement/req-final.md:53:- **JSON（verify --change，GREEN/GAPS 恒在）**：`modifiedIntegrity: [ { file, name, retained: [{id, title}], titleChanged: [{id, oldTitle, newTitle}], dropped: [{id, title}], added: [{id, title}], ambiguous: [{key, side: 'old'|'new'|'both', oldCount, newCount}], missingLines: [{scenario: <id|title|null>, line: <完整原文，不截断> }] } ]`。取值裁定（REQ-3 r2）：`file` = store 相对 suffix（与 projection.modules 同制）；`retained.title` 取**新块**原始标题（规范化仅用于配对判定）；`ambiguous.key` = ID 键取 ID，标题键取规范化标题；每个 MODIFIED 操作恒一条（含全空 diff 条目）；排序：条目按 (file,name)；retained/titleChanged/dropped 按旧块出现序，added 按新块出现序；**ambiguous**：o>0 的键按旧块首次出现序在前，o=0 的 new-only 键按新块首次出现序追加其后；missingLines 按旧块行序。无 ID 场景 id=null 以 title 表达。

# spec-review-v1 — modified-block-integrity

## 正式问题

### SPEC-1：idempotent MODIFIED 的 JSON 契约与引擎算法冲突

`archive-merge/spec.md`、req-final B3/AC5 和 SR-66 要求 identical trimmed replacement 仍产生 entry，但其 diff arrays 全空。design G1 却无 identical fast path，直接执行基数分类；一个包含 `KV-01` 的相同块会自然得到 `retained=[KV-01]`，并非全空。

这还影响 archive 是否需要调用 matcher，以及 literal `out.push(formatIntegrityHuman(...))` 在 formatter 返回空串时是否额外打印空行。

**风险**

实现者只能自行选择两套互斥行为，导致 AC5 deep oracle、SR-66、archive byte compatibility 返工。

**建议**

明确唯一规则。若维持“全空”，在结构扫描和 matcher 前增加：

```text
oldBlock.trim() === newBlock.trim()
→ retained/titleChanged/dropped/added/ambiguous/missingLines 全为 []
```

同时断言 formatter 空结果不会向 archive stdout 增加空行。

### SPEC-2：archive 既不能保证真实“写入前打印”，报告插入点也早于完整 preflight

design G3 把 `out.push(formatIntegrityHuman(...))` 视作打印，但 State A 的 `archiveChange()` 只缓冲 `out[]`；`cli()` 在 `archiveChange()` 完成全部 stage/commit/move 后才执行 `console.log`。因此 `--write` 下报告实际在写入后才对外可见。

此外，design 指定在 `preflightFailures` 为空后生成报告；当前代码随后才检查：

- pre-existing `.tmp-archive`；
- explicit `--changes-dir` destination containment。

按设计直接实现，这两种 `FAILED PREFLIGHT` 仍会携带 integrity section，违反 B3 的“任一 preflight failure 无报告”。

**风险**

AM-46 的时序保证无法实现；失败运行可能展示一份看似可执行的报告。若用户依赖写前日志进行审计，store 已经提交后才看到报告。

**建议**

先裁定“before write”是实际 I/O 时序还是仅 stdout 文本顺序。若是实际时序，需要 streaming emitter 或 prepare/commit 分层，不能只向 `out[]` 入队。报告必须位于所有 temp/destination guards 之后、第一次 `writeFileSync` 之前。AM-46 增加：

- pre-existing temp；
- escaping archive destination；
- emitter 回调中检查 store bytes 尚未变化；
- stage/commit failure 后报告已先行发出的时序 oracle。

### SPEC-3：测试无法证明两个关键 `modifiedBlocks` 捕获点

design G1 正确要求 rename-then-modify 在 rename key swap 前捕获原始 A block，并覆盖 idempotent unchanged。但现有场景不能证明这些要求：

- AM-45 只断言 `missingLines=[]`；由于 requirement heading 本来就不比较，在 rename 后捕获的 G block 也会通过。
- SR-66 未指定 State A 的 stamped rerun repair 路径。该路径在 `buildProjection()` 的 `probe` 后直接 `continue`，最容易漏掉 `modifiedBlocks` entry。
- 没有场景直接检查 `buildProjection.modifiedBlocks` 的 raw text。

**风险**

实现可通过全部报告级场景，却返回错误 baseline 或在 repaired rerun 中漏 entry，破坏“每个 MODIFIED 恰一条”和单快照契约。

**建议**

增加直接 module oracle：

- `RENAMED A->G + MODIFIED G`：`name=G`，`oldBlock` deep-equal rename 前完整 A block，`newBlock` deep-equal delta block；
- stamped base mismatch 且全部操作已应用：`repaired` 分支仍含一条等文本 pair，verify JSON 含全空 entry；
- 普通 trim-equal unchanged 路径同样覆盖。

### SPEC-4：bin-seam 没有可实现的唯一接口

design G3 要求 bin 从 `spec-runner` 取得 `resolveIdPattern+makeIdMatcher` 并注入 archive，但 State A 中：

- `spec-runner` 只导出 `makeIdMatcher`，不导出 `resolveIdPattern`；
- `archive-merge.cli(argv)` 没有 dependency 参数；
- `bin/apriori.js` 仅调用 `archiveMerge.cli(rest)`；
- optional factory 缺席时的行为仅存在于 design，没有 SPEC/module API 契约。

**风险**

实现可能改成 `archive-merge` 反向 require `spec-runner`、提前解析 config、遗漏 high-level CLI 的注入，或使 programmatic `archiveChange()` 调用静默降级，破坏依赖方向和 AM-47。

**建议**

在 design/SPEC 钉死接口，例如：

```js
archiveMerge.cli(argv, { idMatcherFactory })
archiveChange({ ..., idMatcherFactory })
```

bin 注入 lazy factory；`resolveIdPattern` 明确从 `lib/config` 导入或明确新增 `spec-runner` export。factory 只在完整 preflight 成功且存在 MODIFIED entries 时调用，并定义 missing factory 的 module-level 行为。

### SPEC-5：新 human 输出允许 terminal/log control injection

B4 只要求 human 行截断；`missingLines.line`、scenario title、requirement name、ambiguous title key、suffix 都可能来自 repository/change 输入，并将被直接插入 stdout。控制字符尤其是 ANSI `ESC`、换行型文件名等可伪造日志、改写终端显示或隐藏后续 `RESULT`。

warning 已要求整行经过 `sanitizeMsg`，但主要 integrity section 没有同等保护。JSON 经 `JSON.stringify` 会转义控制字符，问题集中在 human path。

**风险**

恶意或损坏的 change/store 内容可污染 CI 日志和操作者终端；这是本 change 新增的外部输入输出通道。

**建议**

为 human formatter 定义统一的 safe rendering：所有外部字段先将 C0/DEL 控制字符替换为 `·`，再执行 120 UTF-16 unit 截断；JSON 继续保留完整原文。增加 title、body line、file suffix 分别携带 `ESC`/control character 的 exact oracle。

## 外部共享状态

没有新增持久化 shared state。archive store 的三个时刻仍为：首次 `--write` 创建、temp+rename 全量更新、无 cleanup；verify 仍为只读加 test child spawn。SPEC-2 涉及的是既有 store mutation 前后的输出时序，不是新的持久状态。

## Advisories

### ADV-1（P0）：State A 的两批 matcher 与 deep JSON 护栏可保持

分段方案只要保证 binding accumulation 仅消费 projection pairs、sibling/integrity 各自按精确 slice 取回 IDs，就不会增加第三批。既有 SR-63 应继续断言总计恰好两批；SR-64 的 GREEN/GAPS full oracle需仅新增预期的 `modifiedIntegrity: []`，其余字段保持 deep-equal。

### ADV-2（P0）：human golden 应由规范先给出完整语法

当前 B4 规定了前缀、计数和截断，但没有给出 entry header、titleChanged、ambiguous、missingLines 的完整文本模板。建议先在 SPEC 固定模板，再制作 full-class golden，避免 golden 反向成为事实规范。

## Ledger delta

| ID | Issue | Risk | Round found | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| SPEC-1 | identical MODIFIED 的全空 entry 契约与无条件基数分类冲突。 | med | STEP2·r1 | open |
| SPEC-2 | archive 缓冲输出无法满足真实写前打印，且设计插入点早于 temp/destination preflight guards。 | high | STEP2·r1 | open |
| SPEC-3 | AM-45/SR-66 无法证明 rename 前 raw capture 与 repaired-rerun entry。 | med | STEP2·r1 | open |
| SPEC-4 | bin→cli→archiveChange 的 matcher DI 接口及 missing-factory 行为未定义，且所称 export 在 State A 不存在。 | med | STEP2·r1 | open |
| SPEC-5 | integrity human section 直接输出外部文本，缺少 control-character sanitization。 | high | STEP2·r1 | open |
